# हिन्दी — Hindi / सुनन अबू दावूद — Sunan Abi Dawud

## Narration 1901

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1906)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ يَعْنِي ابْنَ بِلاَلٍ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْوَهَّابِ الثَّقَفِيُّ، - الْمَعْنَى وَاحِدٌ - عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَلَّى الظُّهْرَ وَالْعَصْرَ بِأَذَانٍ وَاحِدٍ بِعَرَفَةَ وَلَمْ يُسَبِّحْ بَيْنَهُمَا وَإِقَامَتَيْنِ وَصَلَّى الْمَغْرِبَ وَالْعِشَاءَ بِجَمْعٍ بِأَذَانٍ وَاحِدٍ وَإِقَامَتَيْنِ وَلَمْ يُسَبِّحْ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ هَذَا الْحَدِيثُ أَسْنَدَهُ حَاتِمُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ فِي الْحَدِيثِ الطَّوِيلِ وَوَافَقَ حَاتِمَ بْنَ إِسْمَاعِيلَ عَلَى إِسْنَادِهِ مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ الْجُعْفِيُّ عَنْ جَعْفَرٍ عَنْ أَبِيهِ عَنْ جَابِرٍ إِلاَّ أَنَّهُ قَالَ فَصَلَّى الْمَغْرِبَ وَالْعَتَمَةَ بِأَذَانٍ وَإِقَامَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1906]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1906" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ja’far bin Muhammad reported on the authority of his father The Prophet (ﷺ) prayed the noon and the afternoon prayers with one adhan and two iqamahs at ‘Arafah and he did not offer supererogatory prayers between them. He prayed the sunset and night prayers at Al Muzdalifah with one adhan and two iqamahs and he did not offer supererogatory prayers between them. Abu Dawud said This tradition has been narrated by Hatim bin Isma’il as a part of the lengthy tradition. Muhammad bin ‘Ali Al Ju’fi narrated it from Ja’far from his father on the authority of Jabir, like the tradition transmitted by Hatim bin Isma’il. But this version has He offered the sunset and night prayers with one adhan and one iqamah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1906]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1906" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाफ़र बिन मुहम्मद अपने वालिद मुहम्मद बाक़िर से रिवायत करते हैं के नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अरफ़ात में ज़ोहर और अस्र एक अज़ान से पढ़ी, उनके दरमियान कोई नफ़्ल नहीं पढ़ी, अलबत्ता इक़ामत दो थीं, और मग़रिब ओ इशा जम (मुज़दलफ़ाह) में एक अज़ान और दो इक़ामतों से पढ़ी, उनके दरमियान भी कोई नफ़्ल नहीं पढ़ी।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1906]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1906" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جعفر بن محمد اپنے والد محمد باقر سے روایت کرتے ہیں کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے عرفات میں ظہر اور عصر ایک اذان سے پڑھی، ان کے درمیان کوئی نفل نہیں پڑھی، البتہ اقامت دو تھیں، اور مغرب و عشاء جمع ( مزدلفہ ) میں ایک اذان اور دو اقامتوں سے پڑھی، ان کے درمیان بھی کوئی نفل نہیں پڑھی۔ ابوداؤد کہتے ہیں: اس حدیث کو حاتم بن اسماعیل نے ایک لمبی حدیث میں مسنداً بیان کیا ہے اور حاتم بن اسماعیل کی طرح اسے محمد بن علی الجعفی نے جعفر سے، اور جعفر نے اپنے والد محمد سے، محمد نے جابر رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت کیا ہے البتہ اس میں یہ الفاظ ہیں کہ آپ نے مغرب اور عشاء ایک اذان اور ایک اقامت سے پڑھی ۱؎ ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1906]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1906" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jafar bin Muhammad apne walid Muhammad Baqir se riwayat karte hain ke Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Arafat mein Zohar aur Asr ek azan se padhi, unke darmiyan koi nafl nahi padhi, albata iqamat do thin, aur Maghrib o Isha jam (Muzdalifah) mein ek azan aur do iqamaton se padhi, unke darmiyan bhi koi nafl nahi padhi.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1906]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1906" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1902

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1907)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا جَعْفَرٌ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، قَالَ ثُمَّ قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ قَدْ نَحَرْتُ هَا هُنَا وَمِنًى كُلُّهَا مَنْحَرٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَوَقَفَ بِعَرَفَةَ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ قَدْ وَقَفْتُ هَا هُنَا وَعَرَفَةُ كُلُّهَا مَوْقِفٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَوَقَفَ فِي الْمُزْدَلِفَةِ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ قَدْ وَقَفْتُ هَا هُنَا وَمُزْدَلِفَةُ كُلُّهَا مَوْقِفٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1907]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1907" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir said then the Prophet (ﷺ) said “I sacrificed here and the whole of Mina is the place of sacrifice”. He stationed at ‘Arafah and said “I stationed here and the whole of ‘Arafah is the place of station”. He stationed at Al Muzadalifah and said “I stationed here and the whole of Al Muzadalifah is the place of station.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1907]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1907" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">जाबिर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं के फिर नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मैं ने तो यहाँ (मिना में) नह्र किया है और मिना पूरा का पूरा नह्र करने की जगह है, और अरफ़ात में वक़ूफ़ किया तो फ़रमाया: मैं ने यहाँ वक़ूफ़ किया है लेकिन पूरा मैदाने अरफ़ात वक़ूफ़ (ठहरने) की जगह है, और मुज़दलफ़ाह में वक़ूफ़ तो फ़रमाया: मैं यहाँ ठहरा हूँ लेकिन पूरा मुज़दलफ़ाह वक़ूफ़ (ठहरने) की जगह है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1907]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1907" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">جابر رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ پھر نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: میں نے تو یہاں ( منیٰ میں ) نحر کیا ہے اور منیٰ پورا کا پورا نحر کرنے کی جگہ ہے ، اور عرفات میں وقوف کیا تو فرمایا: میں نے یہاں وقوف کیا ہے لیکن پورا میدان عرفات وقوف ( ٹھہرنے ) کی جگہ ہے ، اور مزدلفہ میں وقوف تو فرمایا: میں یہاں ٹھہرا ہوں لیکن پورا مزدلفہ وقوف ( ٹھہرنے ) کی جگہ ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1907]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1907" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke phir Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Main ne to yahan (Mina mein) nahr kiya hai aur Mina pura ka pura nahr karne ki jagah hai, aur Arafat mein waqoof kiya to farmaya: Main ne yahan waqoof kiya hai lekin pura maidane Arafat waqoof (theherne) ki jagah hai, aur Muzdalifah mein waqoof to farmaya: Main yahan thehra hoon lekin pura Muzdalifah waqoof (theherne) ki jagah hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1907]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1907" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1904

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1909)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1909]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1909" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1909</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1909 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ سَعِيدٍ الْقَطَّانُ، عَنْ جَعْفَرٍ، حَدَّثَنِي أَبِي، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، فَذَكَرَ هَذَا الْحَدِيثَ وَأَدْرَجَ فِي الْحَدِيثِ عِنْدَ قَوْلِهِ ‏{‏ وَاتَّخِذُوا مِنْ مَقَامِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ مُصَلًّى ‏}‏ قَالَ فَقَرَأَ فِيهَا بِالتَّوْحِيدِ وَ ‏{‏ قُلْ يَا أَيُّهَا الْكَافِرُونَ ‏}‏ وَقَالَ فِيهِ قَالَ عَلِيٌّ - رضى الله عنه - بِالْكُوفَةِ قَالَ أَبِي هَذَا الْحَرْفُ لَمْ يَذْكُرْهُ جَابِرٌ فَذَهَبْتُ مُحَرِّشًا ‏.‏ وَذَكَرَ قِصَّةَ فَاطِمَةَ رضى الله عنها ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1909" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Jabir (companion). This numbered source entry supplies an abbreviated transmission or variant. Its wording and stated attribution are retained without reconstructing the omitted report or inferring a cross-edition number.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The tradition has also been transmitted by Jabir through a different chain of narrators. He narrated this tradition and added the words “he recited in two rak’ahs the surah relating to Unity of Allaah” and “Say, O disbelievers” to the Qur’anic verse “And take the station of Abraham as a place of prayer. “. This version has ‘Ali said in Kufah. The narrator said “My father said Jabir did not say these words. I went to complain (against Fatimah). He then narrated the story of Fatimah.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1909]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1909" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इस सनद से भी जाबिर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से यही हदीस मरवी है अलबत्ता इस में याक़ूब ने &#x27;वत्तख़िज़ू मिन मक़ामी इब्राहिमा मुसल्ला&#x27; पढ़ने के बाद यह इंदराज किया है के आप ने उन दोनों रकअतों में तौहीद (यानी क़ुल हुवल्लाहु अहद) और क़ुल या-अय्युहल काफ़िरून पढ़ी, और इस में यह इंदराज भी किया के अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कूफ़ा में कहा...</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1909]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1909" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1909 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اس سند سے بھی جابر رضی اللہ عنہ سے یہی حدیث مروی ہے البتہ اس میں یعقوب نے «واتخذوا من مقام إبراهيم مصلى» پڑھنے کے بعد یہ ادراج کیا ہے کہ آپ نے ان دونوں رکعتوں میں توحید ( یعنی «قل هو الله أحد» ) اور «قل يا أيها الكافرون» پڑھیں ، اور اس میں یہ ادراج بھی کیا کہ علی رضی اللہ عنہ نے کوفہ میں کہا یعقوب کہتے ہیں کہ میرے والد نے فرمایا: جابر رضی اللہ عنہ نے علی رضی اللہ عنہ کا یہ قول ذکر نہیں کیا: ( میں نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس فاطمہ رضی اللہ عنہا کے خلاف غصہ دلانے چلا ) ، اور یعقوب نے فاطمہ رضی اللہ عنہا کا واقعہ ذکر کیا، میرے والد جعفر کا کہنا ہے کہ جابر رضی اللہ عنہ نے اس حرف یعنی «فذهبت محرشا» کا ذکر نہیں کیا ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1909" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Is sanad se bhi Jabir (Radi Allahu Anhu) se yahi hadith marwi hai albata is mein Yaqub ne &#x27;Wattakhizu min maqami Ibrahima musalla&#x27; padhne ke baad yeh idraj kiya hai ke aap ne un dono rakaton mein Tauheed (yani Qul huwallahu Ahad) aur Qul ya-ayyuhal kafirun padhi, aur is mein yeh idraj bhi kiya ke Ali (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Kufa mein kaha...</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا هَنَّادٌ، عَنْ أَبِي مُعَاوِيَةَ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ كَانَتْ قُرَيْشٌ وَمَنْ دَانَ دِينَهَا يَقِفُونَ بِالْمُزْدَلِفَةِ وَكَانُوا يُسَمَّوْنَ الْحُمْسَ وَكَانَ سَائِرُ الْعَرَبِ يَقِفُونَ بِعَرَفَةَ قَالَتْ فَلَمَّا جَاءَ الإِسْلاَمُ أَمَرَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى نَبِيَّهُ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ يَأْتِيَ عَرَفَاتٍ فَيَقِفَ بِهَا ثُمَّ يُفِيضَ مِنْهَا فَذَلِكَ قَوْلُهُ تَعَالَى ‏{‏ ثُمَّ أَفِيضُوا مِنْ حَيْثُ أَفَاضَ النَّاسُ ‏}‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1910" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Extract expressly Prophet-directed divine instruction and supplied Quran support; preceding pre-Islamic group practices remain source notes.</p>

<p dir="ltr">When Islam came, Allaah the most High commanded His Prophet (ﷺ) to go to ‘Arafah and station there then go quickly from it. That is in accordance with the words of Him Who is exalted “Then go quickly from where the people went quickly.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1910]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1910" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1910</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">A’ishah said “Quraish and those who followed their religion used to station at Al Muzdalifah and they were called Al Hums and the rest of Arabs used to station at ‘Arafah. </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1910" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयेशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं के क़ुरैश और उनके हम-मज़हब लोग मुज़दलीफः में ही ठहरते थे और लोग उन्हें &#x27;हंस&#x27; यानि बहादुर ओ शुजा कहते थे जबकि सारे अरब के लोग अराफ़ात में ठहरते थे तो अल्लाह त&#x27;आला ने अपने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को अराफात आने और वहां ठहरने फिर वहां से मुज़दलीफः लौटने का हुकुम दिया और यही हुकुम इस आयत में है: &#x27;सुम्मा आफीदू मं हैसु अफादान्नास&#x27; (सौराह आल-बक़रह: १९९) तुम लोग वहाँ से लौटे जहां से सभी लोग लौट-ते हैं - यानि अराफ़ात से.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ قریش اور ان کے ہم مذہب لوگ مزدلفہ میں ہی ٹھہرتے تھے اور لوگ انہیں حمس یعنی بہادر و شجاع کہتے تھے جب کہ سارے عرب کے لوگ عرفات میں ٹھہرتے تھے، تو جب اسلام آیا تو اللہ تعالیٰ نے اپنے نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو عرفات آنے اور وہاں ٹھہرنے پھر وہاں سے مزدلفہ لوٹنے کا حکم دیا اور یہی حکم اس آیت میں ہے: «‏‏‏‏ثم أفيضوا من حيث أفاض الناس» ( سورۃ البقرہ: ۱۹۹ ) تم لوگ وہاں سے لوٹو جہاں سے سبھی لوگ لوٹتے ہیں – یعنی عرفات سے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1910" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ummul Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ke Quraish aur unke hum-mazhab log Muzdalifah mein hi thehrte the aur log unhein &#x27;Hums&#x27; yani bahadur o shuja kehte the jabke saare Arab ke log Arafat mein thehrte the, to jab Islam aaya to Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne apne Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko Arafat aane aur wahan theherne phir wahan se Muzdalifah lautne ka hukum diya aur yahi hukum is aayat mein hai: &#x27;Summa afeedoo min haisu afadannas&#x27; (Surah Al-Baqarah: 199) tum log wahan se lauto jahan se sabhi log laut-te hain - yani Arafat se.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1910" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1906

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1911)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا زُهَيْرُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الأَحْوَصُ بْنُ جَوَّابٍ الضَّبِّيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا عَمَّارُ بْنُ رُزَيْقٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ الأَعْمَشِ، عَنِ الْحَكَمِ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ صَلَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الظُّهْرَ يَوْمَ التَّرْوِيَةِ وَالْفَجْرَ يَوْمَ عَرَفَةَ بِمِنًى ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1911]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1911" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abdullah ibn Abbas: The Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) offered the noon prayer on the 8th of Dhul-Hijjah (Yawm at-Tarwiyah) and dawn prayer on the 9th of Dhul-Hijjah (Yawm al-Arafah) in Mina</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1911]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने यूं-ुत-तरवियाह (आठवी ज़िल्हाज) को ज़ोहर और यौं-इ-अरफ़ह (नौवी ज़िल्हाज) को फ़जर मीणा में पढ़ी.</p>

## Narration 1907

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1912)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، حَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ الأَزْرَقُ، عَنْ سُفْيَانَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ بْنِ رُفَيْعٍ، قَالَ سَأَلْتُ أَنَسَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ قُلْتُ أَخْبِرْنِي بِشَىْءٍ، عَقَلْتَهُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَيْنَ صَلَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الظُّهْرَ يَوْمَ التَّرْوِيَةِ فَقَالَ بِمِنًى ‏.‏ قُلْتُ فَأَيْنَ صَلَّى الْعَصْرَ يَوْمَ النَّفْرِ قَالَ بِالأَبْطَحِ ثُمَّ قَالَ افْعَلْ كَمَا يَفْعَلُ أُمَرَاؤُكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1912]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1912" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">‘Abd Al ‘Aziz bin Rufai’ said I asked Anas bin Malik saying “Tell me something you knew about the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) viz where he offered the noon prayer on Yawm Al Tarwiyah(8th of Dhu Al Hijjah). He replied, In Mina I asked Where did he pray the afternoon prayer on Yaum Al Nafr(12th or 13th of Dhu Al Hijjah). He replied In al-Abtah he then said “Do as your commanders do.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1912]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1912" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल अज़ीज़ बिन राफ़े कहते हैं की मैं ने अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल किया और कहा: मुझे आप वोह बताइये जो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से आप को याद हो की आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने ज़ोहर यौम-उत-तरवियह (आठवीं ज़िलहज्ज) को कहाँ पढ़ी? उन्होंने कहा: मिना में, मैं ने अर्ज़ किया तो लौटने के दिन आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अस्र कहाँ पढ़ी? फ़रमाया: अबतह में, फिर कहा: तुम वही करो जो तुम्हारे उमरा करें।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1912]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1912" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالعزیز بن رفیع کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے انس بن مالک رضی اللہ عنہ سے سوال کیا اور کہا: مجھے آپ وہ بتائیے جو رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے آپ کو یاد ہو کہ آپ نے ظہر یوم الترویہ ( آٹھویں ذی الحجہ ) کو کہاں پڑھی؟ انہوں نے کہا: منیٰ میں، میں نے عرض کیا تو لوٹنے کے دن آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے عصر کہاں پڑھی؟ فرمایا: ابطح میں، پھر کہا: تم وہی کرو جو تمہارے امراء کریں ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1912]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1912" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdul Aziz bin Rafee kehte hain ki main ne Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) se sawal kiya aur kaha: Mujhe aap woh batayiye jo Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se aap ko yaad ho ki aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Zohar yaum-ut-tarwiyah (aathvi Zilhaj) ko kahan parhi? Unhone kaha: Mina mein, main ne arz kiya toh lautne ke din aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Asar kahan parhi? Farmaya: Abtah mein, phir kaha: Tum wahi karo jo tumhare umara karein.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1912]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي، عَنِ ابْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، حَدَّثَنِي نَافِعٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ غَدَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنْ مِنًى حِينَ صَلَّى الصُّبْحَ صَبِيحَةَ يَوْمِ عَرَفَةَ حَتَّى أَتَى عَرَفَةَ فَنَزَلَ بِنَمِرَةَ وَهِيَ مَنْزِلُ الإِمَامِ الَّذِي يَنْزِلُ بِهِ بِعَرَفَةَ حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ عِنْدَ صَلاَةِ الظُّهْرِ رَاحَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مُهَجِّرًا فَجَمَعَ بَيْنَ الظُّهْرِ وَالْعَصْرِ ثُمَّ خَطَبَ النَّاسَ ثُمَّ رَاحَ فَوَقَفَ عَلَى الْمَوْقِفِ مِنْ عَرَفَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1913" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Prophet’s Mina-to-Namrah journey reported by Ibn Umar. Read complete Arafah journey and prayer narrative. The standalone current-place explanatory sentence is source context, separate from reported actions. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Prophet’s combined prayers, address and station at Arafah in Ibn Umar’s account. Read complete Arafah journey and prayer narrative. The standalone current-place explanatory sentence is source context, separate from reported actions.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Umar said the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) proceeded from Mina when he offered the dawn prayer on Yaum Al ‘Arafah (9th of Dhu Al Hijjah) in the morning till he came to ‘Arafah and he descended at Namrah. </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">When the time of the noon prayer came, the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) proceeded earlier and combined the noon and afternoon prayers. He then addressed the people (i.e., recited the sermon) and proceeded. He stationed at a place of stationing in ‘Arafah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1913]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1913" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) yaum-e-Arafah (nauvi Zilhaj) ko Fajar parh kar Mina se chale yahan tak ki Arafat aaye toh Namrah mein utre aur Namrah Arafat mein imam ke utarne ki jagah hai, jab Zohar ka waqt aane ko hua toh Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) (Namrah se) ain dopahar mein chale aur Zohar aur Asar ko jama kiya, phir logon mein khutba diya, phir chale aur Arafat mein mauqif mein waqoof kiya.</p>

## Narration 1909

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1914)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا وَكِيعٌ، حَدَّثَنَا نَافِعُ بْنُ عُمَرَ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ حَسَّانَ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، قَالَ لَمَّا أَنْ قَتَلَ الْحَجَّاجُ ابْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَرْسَلَ إِلَى ابْنِ عُمَرَ أَيَّةُ سَاعَةٍ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَرُوحُ فِي هَذَا الْيَوْمِ قَالَ إِذَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ رُحْنَا ‏.‏ فَلَمَّا أَرَادَ ابْنُ عُمَرَ أَنْ يَرُوحَ قَالُوا لَمْ تَزِغِ الشَّمْسُ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَزَاغَتْ قَالُوا لَمْ تَزِغْ - أَوْ زَاغَتْ - قَالَ فَلَمَّا قَالُوا قَدْ زَاغَتِ ‏.‏ ارْتَحَلَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abdullah ibn Umar: When al-Hajjaj killed Ibn Zubayr, he sent a message to Ibn Umar asking him: At which moment the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) used to proceed (to Arafat) this day? He replied: When it happens so, we shall proceed. When Ibn Umar intended to proceed, the people said: The sun did not decline. He (Ibn Umar) asked: Did it decline? They replied: It did not decline. When they said that the sun had declined, he proceeded</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं की जब हज्जाज ने अब्दुल्लाह बिन ज़ुबैर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) को क़त्ल किया तो इब्न उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) के पास (पूछने के लिए आदमी) भेजा की रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) आज (यानी अरफ़ाह) के दिन (नमाज़ और ख़ुत्बे के लिए) किस वक़्त निकलते थे? उन्होंने जवाब दिया जब यह वक़्त आयेगा तो हम ख़ुद चलेंगे, फिर जब इब्न उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने चलने का इरादा किया तो लोगों ने कहा: अभी सूरज ढला नहीं, उन्होंने पूछा: क्या अब ढल गया? लोगों ने कहा: अभी नहीं ढला, फिर जब लोगों ने कहा की सूरज ढल गया तो वोह चले।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ جب حجاج نے عبداللہ بن زبیر رضی اللہ عنہما کو قتل کیا تو ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما کے پاس ( پوچھنے کے لیے آدمی ) بھیجا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم آج ( یعنی عرفہ ) کے دن ( نماز اور خطبہ کے لیے ) کس وقت نکلتے تھے؟ انہوں نے جواب دیا جب یہ وقت آئے گا تو ہم خود چلیں گے، پھر جب ابن عمر رضی اللہ عنہما نے چلنے کا ارادہ کیا تو لوگوں نے کہا: ابھی سورج ڈھلا نہیں، انہوں نے پوچھا: کیا اب ڈھل گیا؟ لوگوں نے کہا: ابھی نہیں ڈھلا، پھر جب لوگوں نے کہا کہ سورج ڈھل گیا تو وہ چلے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain ki jab Hajjaj ne Abdullah bin Zubair (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ko qatl kiya toh Ibn Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ke paas (poochne ke liye aadmi) bheja ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aaj (yani Arafah) ke din (namaz aur khutbe ke liye) kis waqt nikalte thay? Unhone jawab diya jab yeh waqt aayega toh hum khud chalenge, phir jab Ibn Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ne chalne ka irada kiya toh logon ne kaha: Abhi suraj dhala nahi, unhone poocha: Kya ab dhal gaya? Logon ne kaha: Abhi nahi dhala, phir jab logon ne kaha ki suraj dhal gaya toh woh chale.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1910

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1915)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا هَنَّادٌ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي زَائِدَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنْ بَنِي ضَمْرَةَ عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَوْ عَمِّهِ قَالَ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَهُوَ عَلَى الْمِنْبَرِ بِعَرَفَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1915]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1915" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

## Narration 1911

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1916)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ دَاوُدَ، عَنْ سَلَمَةَ بْنِ نُبَيْطٍ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنَ الْحَىِّ عَنْ أَبِيهِ، نُبَيْطٍ أَنَّهُ رَأَى النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَاقِفًا بِعَرَفَةَ عَلَى بَعِيرٍ أَحْمَرَ يَخْطُبُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1916]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1916" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

## Narration 1912

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1917)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا هَنَّادُ بْنُ السَّرِيِّ، وَعُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا وَكِيعٌ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَجِيدِ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي الْعَدَّاءُ بْنُ خَالِدِ بْنِ هَوْذَةَ، - قَالَ هَنَّادٌ عَنْ عَبْدِ الْمَجِيدِ أَبِي عَمْرٍو، - قَالَ حَدَّثَنِي خَالِدُ بْنُ الْعَدَّاءِ بْنِ هَوْذَةَ، قَالَ رَأَيْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَخْطُبُ النَّاسَ يَوْمَ عَرَفَةَ عَلَى بَعِيرٍ قَائِمٌ فِي الرِّكَابَيْنِ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ رَوَاهُ ابْنُ الْعَلاَءِ عَنْ وَكِيعٍ كَمَا قَالَ هَنَّادٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1917</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1917 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Al-Adda&#x27; ibn Khalid ibn Hudhah said:I saw the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) on 9 Dhul-Hijjah on a camel standing at the stirrups. Abu Dawud said: Ibn al-&#x27;Ala has reported this tradition from Waki&#x27; as narrated by Hammad</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">खालिद बिन अडडा बिन हौज़े (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं अरफ़ह के दिन मैं ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को एक ऊँट पर दोनों रकबों के दरमियान खड़े हो कर लोगों को खुत्बा देते देखा. अबू दाऊद कहते हैं: इसे इब्न अल-अला ने बाकी से इसी तरह रिवायत किया है जैसे हांनद ने.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">خالد بن عداء بن ہوذہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں عرفہ کے دن میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو ایک اونٹ پر دونوں رکابوں کے درمیان کھڑے ہو کر لوگوں کو خطبہ ۱؎ دیتے دیکھا۔ ابوداؤد کہتے ہیں: اسے ابن العلاء نے وکیع سے اسی طرح روایت کیا ہے جیسے ہناد نے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

Abu Dawood kehte hain: Ise Ibn Al-Ala ne Wakee se isi tarah riwayat kiya hai jaise Hannad ne.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ نُفَيْلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَمْرٍو، - يَعْنِي ابْنَ دِينَارٍ - عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ صَفْوَانَ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ شَيْبَانَ، قَالَ أَتَانَا ابْنُ مِرْبَعٍ الأَنْصَارِيُّ وَنَحْنُ بِعَرَفَةَ فِي مَكَانٍ يُبَاعِدُهُ عَمْرٌو عَنِ الإِمَامِ فَقَالَ أَمَا إِنِّي رَسُولُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم إِلَيْكُمْ يَقُولُ لَكُمْ ‏ &quot;‏ قِفُوا عَلَى مَشَاعِرِكُمْ فَإِنَّكُمْ عَلَى إِرْثٍ مِنْ إِرْثِ أَبِيكُمْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1919" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Speaker expressly identifies himself as Messenger&#x27;s envoy and relays the Prophet&#x27;s instruction; named authorization establishes attribution.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yazid ibn Shayban said:We were in a place of stationing at Arafat which Amr (ibn Abdullah) thought was very far away from where the imam was stationing, when Ibn Mirba&#x27; al-Ansari came to us and told (us): I am a messenger for you from the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ). He tells you: Station where you are performing your devotions for you are an heir to the heritage of Abraham</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1919]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1919" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">यज़ीद बिन शैबान कहते हैं हमारे पास इब्न मुरब्बा अंसारी आये हम अरफ़ात में थे (वोह ऐसी जगह थी जिसे अम्र (अम्र बिन अब्दुल्लाह) इमाम से दूर समझते थे), उन्होंने कहा: मैं रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) का भेजा हुआ क़ासिद हूँ, आप का फ़रमान है की अपने मशाएर (निशानियों की जगहों) पर ठहरो इसीलिए की तुम अपने वालिद इब्राहिम के वारिस हो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1919" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1920 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ كَثِيرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا وَهْبُ بْنُ بَيَانٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبِيدَةُ، حَدَّثَنَا سُلَيْمَانُ الأَعْمَشُ، - الْمَعْنَى - عَنِ الْحَكَمِ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، قَالَ أَفَاضَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنْ عَرَفَةَ وَعَلَيْهِ السَّكِينَةُ وَرَدِيفُهُ أُسَامَةُ وَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ عَلَيْكُمْ بِالسَّكِينَةِ فَإِنَّ الْبِرَّ لَيْسَ بِإِيجَافِ الْخَيْلِ وَالإِبِلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَمَا رَأَيْتُهَا رَافِعَةً يَدَيْهَا عَادِيَةً حَتَّى أَتَى جَمْعًا ‏.‏ زَادَ وَهْبٌ ثُمَّ أَرْدَفَ الْفَضْلَ بْنَ الْعَبَّاسِ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ إِنَّ الْبِرَّ لَيْسَ بِإِيجَافِ الْخَيْلِ وَالإِبِلِ فَعَلَيْكُمْ بِالسَّكِينَةِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَمَا رَأَيْتُهَا رَافِعَةً يَدَيْهَا حَتَّى أَتَى مِنًى ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Messenger with Usamah, reported by Ibn Abbas. Read full Arafat return and Wahb’s actual later-rider variant. Preserve both riders, destinations and repeated speech separately. Report type: transmitted variant. Attribution: Messenger with al-Fadl in Wahb’s variant reported by Ibn Abbas. Read full Arafat return and Wahb’s actual later-rider variant. Preserve both riders, destinations and repeated speech separately.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Abbas said The Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) returned from ‘Arafah preserving a quiet demeanor and he took Usamah up behind him (on the Camel). He said “O people preserve a quiet demeanor for piety does not consist in exciting the Horses and the Camels (i.e., in driving them quickly).” He (Ibn ‘Abbas) said “Thereafter I did not see them raising their hands running quickly till he came to Al Muzdalifah.” </p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">The narrator Wahb added He took Al Fadl bin ‘Abbas up behind him (on the Camel) and said O people piety does not consist in exciting the Horses and the Camels (i.e., in driving them quickly), you must preserve a quiet demeanor”. He (Ibn ‘Abbas) said “Thereafter I did not see them raising their hands till he came to Mina.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Messenger’s calm journey and speech reported by Ibn Abbas. Read full visibly damaged Hindi narrative and both calmness instructions. Missing connectives and Usamah detail are not supplied from English; Fadl and destinations remain as written.</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अराफात से लौटे इत्मिनान और सकीनत तारी थी लोगो! इत्मिनान ओ सकीनत को लाज़िम पकरो इसलिए कि घोड़ों और ऊंटों का दोराना कोई नेकी नहीं है. अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं: मैं ने उन्हें (घोड़ों और ऊंटों को) हाथ उठाये दौरते नहीं देखा यहाँ तक की आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) जैम (मुज़दलीफः) आये वसल्लम) ने अपने साथ फ़ज़्ल बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) को बिठाया और फ़रमाया: लोगो! घोड़ों और ऊंटों को डरना नेकी नहीं है तुम इत्मीनान और सकीनत को लाज़िम पकरो. अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं: फिर मैं ने किसी ऊंट और घोड़े को अपने हाथ उठाये (दौरते) नहीं देखा यहाँ तक की आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) मीणा आये.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم عرفات سے لوٹے، آپ پر اطمینان اور سکینت طاری تھی، آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ردیف اسامہ رضی اللہ عنہ تھے، آپ نے فرمایا: لوگو! اطمینان و سکینت کو لازم پکڑو اس لیے کہ گھوڑوں اور اونٹوں کا دوڑانا کوئی نیکی نہیں ہے ۔ عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں: میں نے انہیں ( گھوڑوں اور اونٹوں کو ) ہاتھ اٹھائے دوڑتے نہیں دیکھا یہاں تک کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم جمع ( مزدلفہ ) آئے، ( وہب کی روایت میں اتنا زیادہ ہے ) : پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنے ساتھ فضل بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کو بٹھایا اور فرمایا: لوگو! گھوڑوں اور اونٹوں کو دوڑانا نیکی نہیں ہے تم اطمینان اور سکینت کو لازم پکڑو ۔ عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں: پھر میں نے کسی اونٹ اور گھوڑے کو اپنے ہاتھ اٹھائے ( دوڑتے ) نہیں دیکھا یہاں تک کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم منیٰ آئے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1920</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1920 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Arafat se laute, aap par itminan aur sakeenat tari thi, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke radeef Usama (Radi Allahu Anhu) thay, aap ne farmaya: Logo! Itminan o sakeenat ko lazim pakro isliye ki ghoron aur oonton ka dorana koi neki nahi hai. Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain: Main ne unhein (ghoron aur oonton ko) hath uthaye daurte nahi dekha yahan tak ki aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Jam (Muzdalifah) aaye, (Wahb ki riwayat mein itna zyada hai): Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apne sath Fazl bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) ko bithaya aur farmaya: Logo! Ghoron aur oonton ko dorana neki nahi hai tum itminan aur sakeenat ko lazim pakro. Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) kehte hain: Phir main ne kisi oont aur ghore ko apne hath uthaye (daurte) nahi dekha yahan tak ki aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Mina aaye.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1916

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1921)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ يُونُسَ، حَدَّثَنَا زُهَيْرٌ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ كَثِيرٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا سُفْيَانُ، - وَهَذَا لَفْظُ حَدِيثِ زُهَيْرٍ - حَدَّثَنَا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ عُقْبَةَ، أَخْبَرَنِي كُرَيْبٌ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ أُسَامَةَ بْنَ زَيْدٍ قُلْتُ أَخْبِرْنِي كَيْفَ، فَعَلْتُمْ - أَوْ صَنَعْتُمْ - عَشِيَّةَ رَدِفْتَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ جِئْنَا الشِّعْبَ الَّذِي يُنِيخُ النَّاسُ فِيهِ لِلْمُعَرَّسِ فَأَنَاخَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَاقَتَهُ ثُمَّ بَالَ - وَمَا قَالَ زُهَيْرٌ أَهْرَاقَ الْمَاءَ - ثُمَّ دَعَا بِالْوَضُوءِ فَتَوَضَّأَ وُضُوءًا لَيْسَ بِالْبَالِغِ جِدًّا قُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ الصَّلاَةَ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الصَّلاَةُ أَمَامَكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَرَكِبَ حَتَّى قَدِمْنَا الْمُزْدَلِفَةَ فَأَقَامَ الْمَغْرِبَ ثُمَّ أَنَاخَ النَّاسُ فِي مَنَازِلِهِمْ وَلَمْ يَحِلُّوا حَتَّى أَقَامَ الْعِشَاءَ وَصَلَّى ثُمَّ حَلَّ النَّاسُ ‏.‏ زَادَ مُحَمَّدٌ فِي حَدِيثِهِ قَالَ قُلْتُ كَيْفَ فَعَلْتُمْ حِينَ أَصْبَحْتُمْ قَالَ رَدِفَهُ الْفَضْلُ وَانْطَلَقْتُ أَنَا فِي سُبَّاقِ قُرَيْشٍ عَلَى رِجْلَىَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1921]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibrahim bin ’Uqabah said “Kuraib told me that he asked Umamah bin Zaid saying tell me how you did in the evening when you rode behind the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ). He said “We came to the valley where the people make their Camels kneel down to take rest at night.” The Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) made his she Camel kneel down and he then urinated. He then called for water for ablution and performed the ablution but he did not perform minutely (but performed lightly). I asked Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ), prayer? He replied “Prayer ahead of you”. He then mounted (the Camel) till we came to Al Muzadalifah. There iqamah for the sunset prayer was called. The people then made their Camels kneel down at their places. The Camels were not unloaded as yet, iqamah for the night prayers was called and he prayed. The people then unloaded the Camels. The narrator Muhammad added in his version of the tradition How did you do when the morning came? He replied Al Fadl rode behind him and I walked on foot among the people of the Quraish who went ahead</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1921]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">कुराएब कहते हैं के मैंने उसामा बिन ज़ैद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पूछा: जिस शाम को आप रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ सवार हो कर आये थे आप ने क्या क्या किया? वोह बोले: हम उस घाटी में आये जहाँ लोग अपने ऊंट रात को क़याम के लिए बिठाते हैं, रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपनी ऊंटनी बिठायी, फिर पेशाब किया, (ज़ुहैर ने यह नहीं कहा के पानी बहाया) फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने वुज़ू का पानी मंगाया और वुज़ू किया, जिस में ज़्यादा मुबालिघा नहीं किया, मैंने अर्ज़ किया: अल्लाह के रसूल! नमाज़? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: नमाज़ आगे चल कर पढ़ेंगे। उसामा कहते हैं: फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) सवार हुए यहाँ तक के मुज़दलफ़ाह आये, वहाँ आप ने मग़रिब पढ़ी फिर लोगों ने अपनी सवारियां अपने ठिकानों पर बिठायीं यहाँ तक के इशा की इक़ामत हुई, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इशा पढ़ी, फिर लोगों ने ऊंटों से अपने बोझ उतारे, (मुहम्मद की रिवायत में इतना ज़्यादा है के कुराएब कहते हैं) फिर मैंने पूछा: सुबह हुई तो आप लोगों ने कैसे किया? उन्होंने कहा: आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ फ़ज़्ल बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) सवार हुए, और मैं पैदल क़ुरैश के लोगों के साथ साथ चला।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1921]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">کریب کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے اسامہ بن زید رضی اللہ عنہما سے پوچھا: جس شام کو آپ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ سوار ہو کر آئے تھے آپ نے کیا کیا کیا؟ وہ بولے: ہم اس گھاٹی میں آئے جہاں لوگ اپنے اونٹ رات کو قیام کے لیے بٹھاتے ہیں، رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنی اونٹنی بیٹھا دی، پھر پیشاب کیا، ( زہیر نے یہ نہیں کہا کہ پانی بہایا ) پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے وضو کا پانی منگایا اور وضو کیا، جس میں زیادہ مبالغہ نہیں کیا، میں نے عرض کیا: اللہ کے رسول! نماز، ( پڑھی جائے ) ؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: نماز آگے چل کر ( پڑھیں گے ) ۔ اسامہ کہتے ہیں: پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سوار ہوئے یہاں تک کہ مزدلفہ آئے، وہاں آپ نے مغرب پڑھی پھر لوگوں نے اپنی سواریاں اپنے ٹھکانوں پر بٹھائیں یہاں تک کہ عشاء کی اقامت ہوئی، آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے عشاء پڑھی، پھر لوگوں نے اونٹوں سے اپنے بوجھ اتارے، ( محمد کی روایت میں اتنا زیادہ ہے کہ کریب کہتے ہیں ) پھر میں نے پوچھا: صبح ہوئی تو آپ لوگوں نے کیسے کیا؟ انہوں نے کہا: آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ساتھ فضل بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما سوار ہوئے، اور میں پیدل قریش کے لوگوں کے ساتھ ساتھ چلا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1921]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Kuraib kehte hain ke maine Usama bin Zaid (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha: Jis shaam ko aap Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath sawar ho kar aaye the aap ne kya kya kiya? Woh bole: Hum us ghaati mein aaye jahan log apne oont raat ko qayam ke liye bithate hain, Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apni oontni bithayi, phir peshab kiya, (Zuhair ne yeh nahi kaha ke paani bahaya) phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne wuzu ka paani mangaya aur wuzu kiya, jis mein zyada mubaligha nahi kiya, maine arz kiya: Allah ke Rasool! Namaz? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Namaz aage chal kar padhenge. Usama kehte hain: Phir aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) sawar hue yahan tak ke Muzdalifa aaye, wahan aap ne Maghrib padhi phir logon ne apni sawariyan apne thikanon par bithayin yahan tak ke Isha ki iqamat hui, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Isha padhi, phir logon ne oonton se apne bojh utare, (Muhammad ki riwayat mein itna zyada hai ke Kuraib kehte hain) phir maine poocha: Subah hui toh aap logon ne kaise kiya? Unhone kaha: Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath Fazl bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) sawar hue, aur main paidal Quraish ke logon ke saath saath chala.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1921]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ آدَمَ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ عَيَّاشٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ عَلِيٍّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي رَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَلِيٍّ، قَالَ ثُمَّ أَرْدَفَ أُسَامَةَ فَجَعَلَ يُعْنِقُ عَلَى نَاقَتِهِ وَالنَّاسُ يَضْرِبُونَ الإِبِلَ يَمِينًا وَشِمَالاً لاَ يَلْتَفِتُ إِلَيْهِمْ وَيَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ السَّكِينَةَ أَيُّهَا النَّاسُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَدَفَعَ حِينَ غَابَتِ الشَّمْسُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1922" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Prophet’s Arafat departure reported by Ali ibn Abi Talib. Read complete English field. Usamah riding, quick-pace source description and calmness instruction retained.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Ali ibn AbuTalib: The Prophet then took up Usamah behind him (on the camel), and drove the camel at a quick pace. The people were beating their camels right and left, but he did not pay attention to them; he was saying: O people, preserve a quiet demeanour. He proceeded (from Arafat) when the sun had set</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1922]</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Prophet’s Arafat departure reported by Ali ibn Abi Talib. Read complete Hindi field independently. Source describes a moderate pace and has damaged connectives; preserve that own-field wording without harmonizing to English quick pace.</p>

<p dir="auto">अली (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि फिर (अरकान-इ-अराफात से फ़राग़त के बाद) आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उसामा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को पीछे सवार कर लिया और दरमियानी चाल से ऊँट हांकने लगे ऊंटों को मार रहे थे आप उनकी तरफ मुतवज्जो नहीं होते थे और फरमाते थे: लोगो! इत्मीनान से चलो (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अराफात से उस वक़्त लौटे जब सूरज डूब गया.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1922]</p>

## Narration 1918

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1923)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سُئِلَ أُسَامَةُ بْنُ زَيْدٍ وَأَنَا جَالِسٌ، كَيْفَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَسِيرُ فِي حَجَّةِ الْوَدَاعِ حِينَ دَفَعَ قَالَ كَانَ يَسِيرُ الْعَنَقَ فَإِذَا وَجَدَ فَجْوَةً نَصَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ هِشَامٌ النَّصُّ فَوْقَ الْعَنَقِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hisham bin ‘Urwah reported on the authority of his father Usamah bin Zaid was asked when I was sitting along with him, how did the Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) travel during the Farewell Pilgrimage when he proceeded from ‘Arafah to Al Muzdalifah? He replied he was travelling at a quick pace and when he found an opening he urged on his Camel. Hisham said “Nass(running or urging on the Camel) is above ‘anaq(going at a quick pace).”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवा कहते हैं के मैं बैठा हुआ था के उसामा बिन ज़ैद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से सवाल किया गया: रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हज्जतुल-विदा में अरफ़ात से लौटते वक़्त कैसे चलते थे? फ़रमाया: तेज़ चाल चलते थे, और जब रस्ता पा जाते तो दौड़ते। हिशाम कहते हैं: &#x27;नस&#x27;, &#x27;उनाक़&#x27; से भी ज़्यादा तेज़ चाल को कहते हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ کہتے ہیں کہ میں بیٹھا ہوا تھا کہ اسامہ بن زید رضی اللہ عنہما سے سوال کیا گیا: رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم حجۃ الوداع میں عرفات سے لوٹتے وقت کیسے چلتے تھے؟ فرمایا: تیز چال چلتے تھے، اور جب راستہ پا جاتے تو دوڑتے۔ ہشام کہتے ہیں: «نص» ، «عنق» سے بھی زیادہ تیز چال کو کہتے ہیں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1923]</p>

Farmaya: Tez chaal chalte the, aur jab rasta pa jaate toh daudte. Hisham kehte hain: &#x27;Nas&#x27;, &#x27;Unaq&#x27; se bhi zyada tez chaal ko kehte hain.</p>

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ، حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي، عَنِ ابْنِ إِسْحَاقَ، حَدَّثَنِي إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ كُرَيْبٍ، مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ عَنْ أُسَامَةَ، قَالَ كُنْتُ رِدْفَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَلَمَّا وَقَعَتِ الشَّمْسُ دَفَعَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Prophet’s sunset departure witnessed by Usamah. Read complete English field. Read complete short riding/departure account.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Usamah said:I rode behind the Prophet(ﷺ) When the sun set Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) returned from ‘Arafah (to Al Muzdalifah)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1924]</p>

## Narration 1920

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A1925)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 11</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1925]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 1925</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   1925 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، عَنْ كُرَيْبٍ، مَوْلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ عَنْ أُسَامَةَ بْنِ زَيْدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَهُ يَقُولُ دَفَعَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم مِنْ عَرَفَةَ حَتَّى إِذَا كَانَ بِالشِّعْبِ نَزَلَ فَبَالَ فَتَوَضَّأَ وَلَمْ يُسْبِغِ الْوُضُوءَ قُلْتُ لَهُ الصَّلاَةَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الصَّلاَةُ أَمَامَكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَرَكِبَ فَلَمَّا جَاءَ الْمُزْدَلِفَةَ نَزَلَ فَتَوَضَّأَ فَأَسْبَغَ الْوُضُوءَ ثُمَّ أُقِيمَتِ الصَّلاَةُ فَصَلَّى الْمَغْرِبَ ثُمَّ أَنَاخَ كُلُّ إِنْسَانٍ بَعِيرَهُ فِي مَنْزِلِهِ ثُمَّ أُقِيمَتِ الْعِشَاءُ فَصَلاَّهَا وَلَمْ يُصَلِّ بَيْنَهُمَا شَيْئًا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Messenger’s Arafat-to-Muzdalifah ablution and prayers reported by Usamah ibn Zayd. Read complete English field. Read full urination, incomplete/complete ablution, deferred prayer and two iqamahs.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Usamah bin Zaid said:The Apostle of Allaah(ﷺ) returned from ‘Arafah. When he came to the mountain path , he alighted, urinated and performed the ablution, but he did not perform it completely. I said to him Prayer? He said “The prayer will be offered ahead of you.” He then mounted. When he reached Al Muzdalifah he alighted performed the ablution, performed it well. Thereafter iqamah for the prayer was called and he offered the sunset prayer. Then everyone made his Camel kneel down at his place. Iqamah was then called for night prayer and he offered it but he did not pray between them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1925]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Messenger’s Arafat-to-Muzdalifah ablution and prayers reported by Usamah ibn Zayd. Read complete Hindi field independently. Read full Kurayb transmission and actual prayer/ablution sequence; no wording borrowed.</p>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के ग़ुलाम कुराएब की रिवायत है के उन्होंने उसामा बिन ज़ैद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को कहते सुना के रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अरफ़ात से लौटे जब घाटी में आये तो उतरे, पेशाब किया और वुज़ू किया लेकिन भरपूर वुज़ू नहीं किया, मैंने आप से अर्ज़ किया: नमाज़? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: नमाज़ आगे चल कर पढ़ेंगे, फिर आप सवार हुए जब मुज़दलफ़ाह पहुँचे तो उतरे वुज़ू किया और अच्छी तरह से वुज़ू किया, फिर नमाज़ की तकबीर कही गयी तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मग़रिब पढ़ी फिर हर शख़्स ने अपने ऊंट को अपने क़याम-गाह में बिठाया फिर इशा की इक़ामत हुई तो आप ने इशा पढ़ी और दरमियान में कुछ नहीं पढ़ा।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1925]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما کے غلام کریب کی روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے اسامہ بن زید رضی اللہ عنہما کو کہتے سنا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم عرفات سے لوٹے جب گھاٹی میں آئے تو اترے، پیشاب کیا اور وضو کیا لیکن بھرپور وضو نہیں کیا، میں نے آپ سے عرض کیا: نماز ( کا وقت ہو گیا ہے ) آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: نماز آگے چل کر پڑھیں گے ، پھر آپ سوار ہوئے جب مزدلفہ پہنچے تو اترے وضو کیا اور اچھی طرح سے وضو کیا، پھر نماز کی تکبیر کہی گئی تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مغرب پڑھی پھر ہر شخص نے اپنے اونٹ کو اپنے قیام گاہ میں بٹھایا پھر عشاء کی اقامت ہوئی تو آپ نے عشاء پڑھی اور درمیان میں کچھ نہیں پڑھا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke ghulam Kuraib ki riwayat hai ke unhone Usama bin Zaid (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko kehte suna ke Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Arafat se laute jab ghaati mein aaye toh utre, peshab kiya aur wuzu kiya lekin bharpoor wuzu nahi kiya, maine aap se arz kiya: Namaz? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Namaz aage chal kar padhenge, phir aap sawar hue jab Muzdalifa pahunche toh utre wuzu kiya aur achi tarah se wuzu kiya, phir namaz ki takbeer kahi gayi toh aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Maghrib padhi phir har shakhs ne apne oont ko apne qayam-gah mein bithaya phir Isha ki iqamat hui toh aap ne Isha padhi aur darmiyan mein kuch nahi padha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1921

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم صَلَّى الْمَغْرِبَ وَالْعِشَاءَ بِالْمُزْدَلِفَةِ جَمِيعًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ حَنْبَلٍ، حَدَّثَنَا حَمَّادُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، بِإِسْنَادِهِ وَمَعْنَاهُ وَقَالَ بِإِقَامَةٍ إِقَامَةٍ جَمَعَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏ قَالَ أَحْمَدُ قَالَ وَكِيعٌ صَلَّى كُلَّ صَلاَةٍ بِإِقَامَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1927" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Al Zuhri (successor). This numbered source entry supplies an abbreviated transmission or variant. Its wording and stated attribution are retained without reconstructing the omitted report or inferring a cross-edition number.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The aforesaid tradition has been transmitted by Al Zuhri through a different chain of narrators. This version adds “Each prayer with an iqamah”. Ahmad reported on the authority of Waki’ “he offered each prayer with a single iqamah.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1927]</p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا شَبَابَةُ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مَخْلَدُ بْنُ خَالِدٍ، - الْمَعْنَى - أَخْبَرَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ عُمَرَ، عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي ذِئْبٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، بِإِسْنَادِ ابْنِ حَنْبَلٍ عَنْ حَمَّادٍ، وَمَعْنَاهُ، قَالَ بِإِقَامَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ لِكُلِّ صَلاَةٍ وَلَمْ يُنَادِ فِي الأُولَى وَلَمْ يُسَبِّحْ عَلَى أَثَرِ وَاحِدَةٍ مِنْهُمَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَخْلَدٌ لَمْ يُنَادِ فِي وَاحِدَةٍ مِنْهُمَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Al Zuhri (successor). This numbered source entry supplies an abbreviated transmission or variant. Its wording and stated attribution are retained without reconstructing the omitted report or inferring a cross-edition number.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The aforesaid tradition has also been transmitted to the same effect by by Al Zuhri with a different chain of narrators beginning with Ibn Hanbal on the authority of Hammad. This version adds “With an iqamah for every prayer, he did not call adhan for the first prayer and he did not offer supererogatory prayer after any of them. The narrator Makhlad said “He did not call adhan for any of them.”</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1928]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इस सनद से भी ज़ुहरी से इब्न हंबल की सनद से उन्होंने हम्मद से उसी मफ़हूम की हदीस रिवायत की है इस में है: हर नमाज़ के लिए एक अलग इक़ामत कही और पहली नमाज़ के लिए अज़ान नहीं दी और न उन दोनों में से किसी के बाद नफल पढ़ी. मखलाड़ कहते हैं: इन दोनों में से किसी नमाज़ के लिए अज़ान नहीं दी.</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Is sanad se bhi Zuhri se Ibn Hanbal ki sanad se unhone Hammad se usi mafhoom ki hadees riwayat ki hai is mein hai: Har namaz ke liye ek alag iqamat kahi aur pehli namaz ke liye azan nahi di aur na un dono mein se kisi ke baad nafal padhi. Makhlad kehte hain: In dono mein se kisi namaz ke liye azan nahi di.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ كَثِيرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، قَالَ صَلَّيْتُ مَعَ ابْنِ عُمَرَ الْمَغْرِبَ ثَلاَثًا وَالْعِشَاءَ رَكْعَتَيْنِ فَقَالَ لَهُ مَالِكُ بْنُ الْحَارِثِ مَا هَذِهِ الصَّلاَةُ قَالَ صَلَّيْتُهُمَا مَعَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي هَذَا الْمَكَانِ بِإِقَامَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1929" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Ibn Umar explicitly identifies the demonstrated prayers and iqamah as what he offered with Messenger in that place; preserve the complete local referent.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah ibn Malik said:I offered three rak&#x27;ahs of the sunset prayer and two rak&#x27;ahs of the night prayer along with Ibn Umar. Thereupon Malik ibn al-Harith said: What is this prayer? He said: I offered these prayers along with the Messenger of Allah (ﷺ) in this place with a single iqamah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1929]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1929" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन मालिक कहते हैं के मैंने इब्न उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के साथ मग़रिब तीन रकअत और इशा दो रकअत पढ़ी, तो मालिक बिन हारिस ने उन से कहा: यह कौन सी नमाज़ है? उन्होंने कहा: मैंने रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के साथ यह दोनों उसी जगह एक तकबीर से पढ़ें।</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Malik kehte hain ke maine Ibn Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke saath Maghrib teen rakaat aur Isha do rakaat padhi, toh Malik bin Haris ne un se kaha: Yeh kaun si namaz hai? Unhone kaha: Maine Rasoolullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke saath yeh dono usi jagah ek takbeer se padhin.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/1929" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ سُلَيْمَانَ الأَنْبَارِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ، - يَعْنِي ابْنَ يُوسُفَ - عَنْ شَرِيكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي إِسْحَاقَ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ، وَعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، قَالاَ صَلَّيْنَا مَعَ ابْنِ عُمَرَ بِالْمُزْدَلِفَةِ الْمَغْرِبَ وَالْعِشَاءَ بِإِقَامَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ فَذَكَرَ مَعْنَى حَدِيثِ ابْنِ كَثِيرٍ ‏.‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Sa’id bin Jubair (successor). This numbered source entry preserves a transmitted historical or narrator account. Its own speaker and source wording are retained; no direct Prophetic speech is inferred.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Sa’id bin Jubair and ‘Abd Allah bin Malik said “We offered the sunset and the night prayers at Al Muzdalifah along with ibn ‘Umar with one iqamah.” The narrator then narrated the rest of the tradition as reported by Ibn Kathir</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 1930]</p>
